Second:

Ruling: representation; project retained. Step 5, question 2 ('Were you self-employed at any time in 2025', i1040gi p. 42) is answered from the return: a Schedule C on the return is self-employment and, within scope, the only kind (partnership, farm, clergy and church employee income are refused). The second asks the same fact as a separate input.

Second transcription's note: Several situations send the filer elsewhere rather than to a result, and each is refused. Form 8862 is refused through household.credit_disallowed. Form 2555 (Step 1, question 3) is refused through sa.excluded_income, which also covers Form 4563 and Puerto Rico, so it is broader than the text. Nonresident aliens (Step 1, question 4) are refused through scope.other_income. Step 2, question 3 (Form 4797) is refused through eic.h_4797_refuse. Step 2, question 4 (Schedule E, rental of personal property, Form 8814, passive activity) is refused through scope.schedule1, scope.other_income, scope.other_line16 and eic.h_passive. The filer may ask the IRS to figure the credit (Step 6), but that is not asked; the credit is always figured.

Ruling: refusal representation; project retained. The project's expansion differs from the second's by the scope gates it carries as refusals on Steps 1 and 2 (scope.schedule1, scope.other_income, scope.other_line16, sa.excluded_income) and the Form 8862 refusal both sides carry (household.credit_disallowed; i1040gi p. 43, Form 8862, who must file); the Step 1 to 5 tests, with the filer's EIC SSN (person.eic_ssn, p. 44) and Worksheet A, appear on both sides.

### eic.check_27c — The instructions say to check the box on line 27c (a STOP that says so, or Worksheet A lin
Status: **DISAGREE**

Second transcription's note: Each STOP counts only when the flow reaches it, so earlier questions must have passed. STOPs that do not mention line 27c are excluded: Step 1, question 1; Step 2; Step 3, question 5; Step 4, questions 1, 2 and 6; Step 5, question 3. The nonresident alien STOP is refused, not encoded.

Ruling: representation and scope; project retained. The project's expansion differs only by the same scope gates as eic.credit; the second writes the line 27c STOPs in its own structure. Both count each STOP that says to check the box (i1040gi p. 40, Step 1, question 2; p. 41, Step 3, question 6; p. 42, Step 4, questions 3 and 5; p. 46, Worksheet A, line 2; p. 47, Worksheet B, line 5) only when the flow reaches it.

Second transcription's note: The table has one column group for single, head of household or qualifying surviving spouse, which married filing separately also uses (★ note), and one group for married filing jointly. It is split into four tables by number of children. Each band is [at least, less than). The starred partial bands are encoded as printed: $0 or $1 or $3 below the limit. Amounts below $1 and at or above the last printed band give $0. The limit tests on eic.h_pass then stop the credit ('you can't take the credit').

Ruling: representation; project retained. Both choose the EIC Table column by the number of qualifying children with valid SSNs; the project adds the guard for an earned income below the table's first row ($1), which has no printed row (ambiguity register amb.2025.eic_below_table).

### 3 — Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b
Status: **AGREE**

### 4 — Are the amounts on lines 3 and 1 the same?
Status: **AGREE**

### 5 — Look up the amount on line 3 in the EIC Table to find the credit, using the column for you
Status: **DISAGREE**

```

Ruling: equivalent representation; project retained. The same choice (line 2 when line 4 is Yes or line 3 is below the line 5 amount; the smaller of lines 5 and 2 otherwise; i1040gi p. 46, Worksheet A, lines 4 to 6) written as two guards instead of nested choices; the operands differ only as the lines they read were ruled.

## Refusal gates listed by the second transcription

Reported, not judged: the project often refuses before a worksheet is used, on the line that chooses it, which a per-line comparison cannot see.

| line | gates listed | not read by the project's line |
| --- | --- | --- |
| eic.credit | eic.clergy, eic.h_4797_refuse, eic.h_passive, eic.h_qc_screen, f8959.unsupported, household.credit_disallowed, sa.excluded_income, scope.other_income, scope.other_line16, scope.schedule1 | eic.clergy, eic.h_4797_refuse, eic.h_passive, eic.h_qc_screen, f8959.unsupported |
| eic.check_27c | eic.clergy, eic.h_qc_screen, f8959.unsupported | eic.clergy, eic.h_qc_screen, f8959.unsupported |
| (Step 5, 1) | eic.clergy, f8959.unsupported | eic.clergy, f8959.unsupported |
| (Step 5, 3) | eic.clergy, f8959.unsupported | eic.clergy, f8959.unsupported |
| (Step 5, 5) | eic.clergy, f8959.unsupported | eic.clergy, f8959.unsupported |
| 4b | eic.clergy, f8959.unsupported | eic.clergy, f8959.unsupported |
| 6 (eic.wsb_6) | eic.clergy, f8959.unsupported | eic.clergy, f8959.unsupported |
| 1 | eic.clergy, f8959.unsupported | eic.clergy, f8959.unsupported |
| 2 | eic.clergy, eic.h_qc_screen, f8959.unsupported | eic.clergy, eic.h_qc_screen, f8959.unsupported |
| 4 | eic.clergy, f8959.unsupported | eic.clergy, f8959.unsupported |
| 5 | eic.h_qc_screen | eic.h_qc_screen |
| 6 (eic.line6) | eic.clergy, eic.h_qc_screen, f8959.unsupported | eic.clergy, eic.h_qc_screen, f8959.unsupported |

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: "eic.line(Step 5, 1)"
    printed: "(Step 5, 1)"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 1z"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line1z}
    refuses: [eic.clergy, f8959.unsupported]
    note: "The Clergy and Church employees rules replace line 1 with line 1z (or 1a) less the amount also on Schedule SE. The tool does not compute that case. A member of the clergy is refused through eic.clergy. Church employee income is refused through the screen f8959.unsupported, which names church employee income."

  - id: eic.clergy
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Member of the clergy filing Schedule SE with an amount also on Form 1040, line 1z"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: input, source: "Line 27b / Step 5, question 1: Are you a minister, a member of a religious order who has not taken a vow of poverty, or a Christian Science practitioner, filing Schedule SE, and does the amount on line 2 of Schedule SE include an amount also reported on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 1z?"}

  - id: "eic.line(Step 5, 2)"
    printed: "(Step 5, 2)"
    label: "Enter the Medicaid waiver payment amounts excluded from income on Schedule 1, line 8s, unless you choose to include these amounts in earned income, in which case enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sales.zero}
    note: "The tool does not support Schedule 1 line 8s (scope.schedule1 and s1.line8_other screen it out), so no Medicaid waiver payments are excluded. This line is zero."

  - id: "eic.line(Step 5, 3)"
    printed: "(Step 5, 3)"
    label: "Subtract line 2 from line 1"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: "eic.line(Step 5, 1)", take: "eic.line(Step 5, 2)"}

  - id: "eic.line(Step 5, 4)"
    printed: "(Step 5, 4)"
    label: "Enter all of your nontaxable combat pay if you elect to include it in earned income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sales.zero}
    note: "Including nontaxable combat pay is an election. The tool does not offer it, so it reads the filer as not electing and enters zero. That is a permitted choice, so the line does not refuse."

  - id: "eic.line(Step 5, 5)"
    printed: "(Step 5, 5)"
    label: "Add lines 3 and 4. This is your earned income"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: ["eic.line(Step 5, 3)", "eic.line(Step 5, 4)"]}

  - id: eic.self_employed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 5, question 2: self-employed (Worksheet B)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: input, source: "Step 5, question 2: Were you self-employed at any time in 2025, or are you filing Schedule SE because you were a member of the clergy or you had church employee income, or are you filing Schedule C as a statutory employee?"}

  - id: eic.h_wsb_part12
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B, lines 1e and 2c (you and your spouse if filing jointly)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: person, field: person.eic_se}

  - id: eic.h_wsb_part3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B, line 3. Enter the amount from Schedule C, line 1, that you are filing as a statutory employee"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: sc
      field: sc.line1
      where: {op: test, eq: [sc.statutory, {const: true}]}
    note: "Line 3 is read as the line 1 total of the Schedules C marked as statutory employee, summed over every person. person.eic_se is taken to exclude statutory employee income, as Part 2 directs."

  - id: eic.line4b
    printed: "4b"
    label: "Worksheet B, line 4b. Combine lines 1e, 2c, 3, and 4a. This is your total earned income"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [eic.h_wsb_part12, eic.h_wsb_part3, "eic.line(Step 5, 5)"]}
    note: "Line 4a ('Enter your earned income from Step 5') is read directly from Step 5, line 5."

  - id: eic.wsb_6
    printed: "6 (eic.wsb_6)"
    label: "Worksheet B, line 6. Enter your total earned income from Part 4, line 4b"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: eic.line4b, take: {const: 0}}

  - id: eic.line1
    printed: "1"
    label: "Enter your earned income from Step 5 (Worksheet A, line 1), or Worksheet B, line 6"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: false}]}
          then: "eic.line(Step 5, 5)"
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.wsb_6
    note: "Worksheet A lines 1 to 6 and Worksheet B lines 6 to 11 are the same computation, so they are encoded once. Line 1 takes Worksheet B, line 6 when Step 5, question 2 is Yes."

  - id: eic.h_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Married filing jointly"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}

  - id: eic.h_qc_screen_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Children who otherwise meet the EIC tests and may be the qualifying child of another person, are married, or fall under the rule for divorced or separated parents"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: dependent
      field: dependent.one
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: [dependent.qc_relationship, {const: true}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [dependent.qc_age, {const: true}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [dependent.us_half, {const: true}]}
          - op: test
            any:
              - {op: test, eq: [dependent.other_claimant, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [dependent.married, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [dependent.divorced_rule, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.h_qc_screen
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "A qualifying-child situation outside this tool (qualifying child of more than one person, married child, divorced or separated parents)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [eic.h_qc_screen_count, {const: 0}]}

  - id: eic.h_qc_ssn_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of qualifying children who have valid SSNs"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: dependent
      field: dependent.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_ssn_child, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [eic.h_qc_screen]

  - id: eic.h_line2_c0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 1, no qualifying children who have valid SSNs"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_0, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}

  - id: eic.h_line2_c1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 1, one qualifying child who has a valid SSN"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_1, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}

  - id: eic.h_line2_c2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 1, two qualifying children who have valid SSNs"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_2, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}

  - id: eic.h_line2_c3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 1, three or more qualifying children who have valid SSNs"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_3, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}

  - id: eic.line2
    printed: "2"
    label: "Look up the amount on line 1 in the EIC Table to find the credit, using the column for your filing status and the number of qualifying children who have valid SSNs"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_ssn_count, {const: 0}]}
          then: eic.h_line2_c0
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_ssn_count, {const: 1}]}
          then: eic.h_line2_c1
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_ssn_count, {const: 2}]}
          then: eic.h_line2_c2
        - guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_qc_ssn_count, {const: 3}]}
          then: eic.h_line2_c3
    note: "The table has one column group for single, head of household or qualifying surviving spouse, which married filing separately also uses (★ note), and one group for married filing jointly. It is split into four tables by number of children. Each band is [at least, less than). The starred partial bands are encoded as printed: $0 or $1 or $3 below the limit. Amounts below $1 and at or above the last printed band give $0. The limit tests on eic.h_pass then stop the credit ('you can't take the credit')."

  - id: eic.line3
    printed: "3"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line11b}

  - id: eic.line4
    printed: "4"
    label: "Are the amounts on lines 3 and 1 the same?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [eic.line3, eic.line1]}

  - id: eic.h_line5_c0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 3, no qualifying children who have valid SSNs"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_0, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}

  - id: eic.h_line5_c1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 3, one qualifying child who has a valid SSN"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_1, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}

  - id: eic.h_line5_c2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 3, two qualifying children who have valid SSNs"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_2, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}

  - id: eic.h_line5_c3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 3, three or more qualifying children who have valid SSNs"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_3, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}

  - id: eic.line5
    printed: "5"
    label: "Look up the amount on line 3 in the EIC Table to find the credit, using the column for your filing status and the number of qualifying children who have valid SSNs"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_ssn_count, {const: 0}]}
          then: eic.h_line5_c0
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_ssn_count, {const: 1}]}
          then: eic.h_line5_c1
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_ssn_count, {const: 2}]}
          then: eic.h_line5_c2
        - guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_qc_ssn_count, {const: 3}]}
          then: eic.h_line5_c3
    note: "Line 5 is computed even when the worksheet says to leave it blank. Line 6 uses it only on the 'No' branch of line 5."

  - id: eic.h_wst0_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$10,620"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold0.other}

  - id: eic.h_wst0_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$17,730"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold0.joint}

  - id: eic.h_wst1_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$23,350"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold1.other}

  - id: eic.h_wst1_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$30,470"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold1.joint}

  - id: eic.h_ws_threshold
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 5 threshold: $10,620 ($17,730 if married filing jointly) with no qualifying children; $23,350 ($30,470) with 1 or more"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_ssn_count, {const: 0}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}]}
          then: eic.h_wst0_other
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_ssn_count, {const: 0}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}]}
          then: eic.h_wst0_joint
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.h_qc_ssn_count, {const: 1}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}]}
          then: eic.h_wst1_other
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.h_qc_ssn_count, {const: 1}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}]}
          then: eic.h_wst1_joint

  - id: eic.h_line3_below
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 5 question: is the amount on line 3 less than the threshold?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, eic.h_ws_threshold]}

  - id: eic.h_use_line2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Enter the amount from line 2 on line 6 (line 4 Yes, or line 5 Yes)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.line4, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_line3_below, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.h_smaller52
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Look at the amounts on lines 5 and 2. Then, enter the smaller amount on line 6"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [eic.line5, eic.line2]}

  - id: eic.line6
    printed: "6 (eic.line6)"
    label: "This is your earned income credit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_use_line2, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.line2
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_use_line2, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.h_smaller52

  - id: eic.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sales.zero}

  - id: eic.h_lim0_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$19,104"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit0.other}

  - id: eic.h_lim0_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$26,214"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit0.joint}

  - id: eic.h_lim1_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$50,434"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit1.other}

  - id: eic.h_lim1_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$57,554"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit1.joint}

  - id: eic.h_lim2_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$57,310"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit2.other}

  - id: eic.h_lim2_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$64,430"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit2.joint}

  - id: eic.h_lim3_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$61,555"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit3.other}

  - id: eic.h_lim3_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$68,675"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit3.joint}

  - id: eic.h_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 1, question 1 and Step 5, question 3 limit for the number of children who have valid SSNs and filing status"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_ssn_count, {const: 0}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim0_other
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_ssn_count, {const: 0}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim0_joint
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_ssn_count, {const: 1}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim1_other
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_ssn_count, {const: 1}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim1_joint
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_ssn_count, {const: 2}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim2_other
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_ssn_count, {const: 2}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim2_joint
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.h_qc_ssn_count, {const: 3}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim3_other
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.h_qc_ssn_count, {const: 3}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim3_joint
    note: "Step 1 counts 'children who have valid SSNs [who] lived with you'. It is read with the same count as Step 5, question 3, which counts qualifying children who have valid SSNs."

  - id: eic.h_step1_agi
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 1, question 1: is the amount on Form 1040, line 11b, less than the limit?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: [f1040.line11b, eic.h_limit]}

  - id: eic.h_7a_pos
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 1040, line 7a (if a loss, enter -0-)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: larger_of, of: [f1040.line7a, eic.h_zero]}

  - id: eic.h_invest
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 2, question 1: Investment income (lines 2a, 2b, 3b and 7a)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line2a, f1040.line2b, f1040.line3b, eic.h_7a_pos]}

  - id: eic.h_invest_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$11,950"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.investment_limit}

  - id: eic.h_invest_over
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 2, question 2: Is your investment income more than $11,950?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [eic.h_invest, eic.h_invest_limit]}

  - id: eic.h_4797_refuse
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 2, question 3: investment income over $11,950 and filing Form 4797 (see Form 4797 filers; Pub. 596 Worksheet 1)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_invest_over, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [sd.other_forms, {const: true}]}
    note: "Filing Form 4797 is read from the screen sd.other_forms, which covers Form 4797."

  - id: eic.h_passive_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedules C in which you did not materially participate"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: sc
      field: sc.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [sc.passive, {const: false}]}

  - id: eic.h_passive
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 2, question 4: income or loss from a passive activity"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [eic.h_passive_count, {const: 0}]}

  - id: eic.h_qc_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Children who meet the conditions to be your qualifying child for the EIC (with or without a valid SSN)"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: dependent
      field: dependent.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_child, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.h_has_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3, question 1: Do you have at least one child who meets the conditions to be your qualifying child for the EIC?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 0}]}

  - id: eic.h_married_sep
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3, question 3 / Step 4, question 1: a married taxpayer whose filing status is married filing separately or head of household"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "hoh"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [household.married, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.h_stop3_5
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3, question 5 STOP: same principal residence for the last 6 months and not legally separated and living apart"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_married_sep, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.same_home, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.legally_separated, {const: false}]}

  - id: eic.h_step3_pass
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3 passed (joint return, or questions 5 and 6 do not stop)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_stop3_5, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: false}]}

  - id: eic.separated_box
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Check the box in the Dependents section for the special rule for separated spouses"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_married_sep, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_stop3_5, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: false}]}

  - id: eic.h_age_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You, or your spouse if filing jointly, born after December 31, 1960, and before January 2, 2001"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: person
      field: person.one
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: [person.born_after_1960, {const: true}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [person.born_before_2001, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.h_age_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4, question 2: at least age 25 but under age 65 at the end of 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [eic.h_age_count, {const: 0}]}
    note: "The direction to see Pub. 596 when a spouse or the filer died in 2025 is not encoded. The birth-date test printed in the question is used."

  - id: eic.h_step4_pass
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4 passed (filers without a qualifying child)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_married_sep, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_age_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.us_home, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: false}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [sa.dependent, {const: false}]}
    note: "Question 6 ('Can you be claimed as a dependent on someone else's return?') is read from the input sa.dependent."

  - id: eic.h_child_pass
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3 passed with a qualifying child, or Step 4 passed without one"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_step3_pass, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_step4_pass, {const: true}]}
    note: "A filer whose qualifying child lacks a valid SSN goes through Step 3 and skips Step 4, as the flowchart says. The table then uses the 0-children column."

  - id: eic.h_wsb_positive
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B, line 4b is more than zero"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [eic.line4b, {const: 0}]}

  - id: eic.h_earned_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 5, question 3 / Worksheet B, line 5: is your earned income less than the limit?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: [eic.line1, eic.h_limit]}

  - id: eic.h_step5_pass
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 5 passed (earned income under the limit; for Worksheet B, line 4b more than zero)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_earned_ok, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_wsb_positive, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.h_reached2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 1 passed"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_step1_agi, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.all_eic_ssn, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.h_reached3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Steps 1 and 2 passed"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_reached2, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_invest_over, {const: false}]}

  - id: eic.h_reached5
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Steps 1 through 4 passed"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_reached3, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_child_pass, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.h_pass
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Steps 1 through 5 passed"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_reached5, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_step5_pass, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.credit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Earned income credit for Form 1040, line 27a"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_pass, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.line6
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_pass, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.h_zero
    refuses: [household.credit_disallowed, sa.excluded_income, scope.other_income, eic.h_4797_refuse, scope.other_line16, scope.schedule1, eic.h_passive]
    note: "Several situations send the filer elsewhere rather than to a result, and each is refused. Form 8862 is refused through household.credit_disallowed. Form 2555 (Step 1, question 3) is refused through sa.excluded_income, which also covers Form 4563 and Puerto Rico, so it is broader than the text. Nonresident aliens (Step 1, question 4) are refused through scope.other_income. Step 2, question 3 (Form 4797) is refused through eic.h_4797_refuse. Step 2, question 4 (Schedule E, rental of personal property, Form 8814, passive activity) is refused through scope.schedule1, scope.other_income, scope.other_line16 and eic.h_passive. The filer may ask the IRS to figure the credit (Step 6), but that is not asked; the credit is always figured."

  - id: eic.h_stop_ssn
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 1, question 2 STOP (no valid SSN); check the box on line 27c"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_step1_agi, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.all_eic_ssn, {const: false}]}

  - id: eic.h_stop_3q6
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3, question 6 STOP (qualifying child of another person); check the box on line 27c"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_reached3, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_stop3_5, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.h_stop_4q3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4, question 3 STOP (main home not in the United States); check the box on line 27c"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_reached3, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_married_sep, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_age_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.us_home, {const: false}]}

  - id: eic.h_stop_4q5
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4, question 5 STOP (qualifying child of another person); check the box on line 27c"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_reached3, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_married_sep, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_age_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.us_home, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.h_stop_wsb4b
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B, line 4b is zero or less STOP; check the box on line 27c"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_reached5, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_wsb_positive, {const: false}]}

  - id: eic.h_stop_wsb5
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B, line 5 No STOP; check the box on line 27c"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_reached5, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_wsb_positive, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_earned_ok, {const: false}]}

  - id: eic.h_stop_line2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet A, line 2 (Worksheet B, line 7) is zero STOP; check the box on line 27c"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_pass, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.line2, {const: 0}]}

  - id: eic.check_27c
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "The instructions say to check the box on line 27c"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_stop_ssn, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_stop_3q6, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_stop_4q3, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_stop_4q5, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_stop_wsb4b, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_stop_wsb5, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_stop_line2, {const: true}]}
    note: "Each STOP counts only when the flow reaches it, so earlier questions must have passed. STOPs that do not mention line 27c are excluded: Step 1, question 1; Step 2; Step 3, question 5; Step 4, questions 1, 2 and 6; Step 5, question 3. The nonresident alien STOP is refused, not encoded."
